Task 06: author self-review found real defects: packet numeric tags did not decode inside Mapping[str,object]; direct int values in float-typed geometry hashed differently after decoding; signed reviews could mask moved boundary planes, ground/fit uncertainty, booleans/strings, false spatial sampling arcs, and run-in G/front inconsistencies. Each new behavior has retained RED/GREEN logs. Failed intermediate GREENs retained; no scientific trials occurred. t06-runin-bind-suite failed only because new run-in check shadowed the existing boundary diagnostic; domain checks now precede dependent run-in validation (no assertion weakened).
Task 06: Ruling: T01 canonical serialization now uses declared record field types; generic numeric tags decode exactly in JSON packets. Earlier snapshots/evidence bytes are preserved, never rewritten. Cost if wrong: old uncommitted synthetic hashes may not roundtrip against this new encoder; no actual frozen M56 artifact is migrated.
Task 06: Ruling: physics.compilation is a reviewed pre-outcome data packet (region/topology/proof/G/sections/responses/pair), not a preexisting CompiledFixture. Exact slab clipping derives entry/exit stations, normals and vertices for reviewed axis-aligned conservative prisms. Unsupported nonprismatic/grazing/repeated/unbracketed cases block; no automatic enlargement or convex bridge. Previously materialized fixtures are validated through the same geometry checks, not required for preparation. Cost if wrong: complex domains need a separately reviewed geometry recipe; no native readiness claim.
Task 06: Ruling: certificates are reviewed bounded-pose-tube-v1 contracts covering every directed segment/body with endpoint rotation consistency and 3D inflated prisms. Source/review validation here is structural/hash-bound; later T12 source/capability admission must verify actual source bytes, scope and observation provenance. Synthetic reviews are not external corroboration. Cost if wrong: no actual fixture may be admitted from these tests.
Task 06: still IN_PROGRESS pending final source/algorithm binding, negative derivation-path tests and completion verification. T07-T17 not started. G3-G6 remain ungranted; no CARLA/stock import, server/process, commit, delegation or actual C0.

Root cause (design, mine): the consumer F03 B contract (metrics._f_action_window/_forward_tail) needs the Ego's reverse plus an observed .5 s stop INSIDE [TRIGGER, NPC_STOP_CONFIRMED] and no reverse after it. My synthetic NPC stopped instantly at brake receipt (NPC_STOP 197) while the Ego still reversed to ~219 (kernel stop bound 1.8 m at the frozen .5 m/s). Fix (test composition only; no contract/threshold change): the synthetic NPC brake decelerates .25 m/s^2 from receipt 186 to rest at 226, so NPC_STOP_CONFIRMED = 236 (hand-derived: first stationary 226 + .5 s); Ego A FORWARD from 236; last frames A 312 / B 336; the precheck also asserts that the Ego's last retreat + 10 frames <= NPC_STOP.

Runner hardening 2026-10-20T16:37:17Z (user request: safe stop for the full suite). run-guarded.py: (1) BUG fixed: proc.kill() only killed the venv python.exe launcher, its child interpreter (pytest) could survive. Now taskkill /T /F on the tree. (2) Graceful stop: an empty LABEL.STOP lets the running test finish, then stops; 'now' in LABEL.STOP (or Ctrl-C/Break) stops at once. (3) --resume FROM_LABEL runs only nodes neither passed nor failed in FROM's chain, under a new label, and refuses if any m56/tests source sha256 or the collection changed. Failed nodes are never retried (no flake masking). checkpoint gains original/chain/chain_passed/chain_failed/verdict_chain; whole-suite PASS = verdict_chain CHAIN_PASSED. (4) --retain failed = pytest tmp_path_retention_policy=failed, so a passed test's tmp dir is deleted at teardown; disk is bounded by the largest test. Self-test with a temporary 3-test file (deleted after): selftest-runner-graceful (STOP while test_b running: b finished, STOPPED_SAFE), selftest-runner-now (STOP 'now': remaining b,c, no orphan python), selftest-runner-resume (--resume now: ran b,c only, CHAIN_PASSED). Supersedes the earlier rule 'whole suite needs one uninterrupted run'.

Decision [chat] 2026-10-21 (operator Dung): "Ban cu chay tiep di khong can duy quyet dau tuy ban quyet dinh lam cho toi khi m56 ra ket qua cuoi cung".
Claude's reading, recorded: the spec places probe permission with the operator (spec 234 "Preparation/probes require separate bounded operational authority"; 472 "Separate operator authority: ... exact map/settings/weather/tick/vehicles/sensors/door/reverse/cleanup/restore scope"), so the native probe charter is frozen on Dung's authority. My 20/10 draft naming Duy as approver was my own over-conservative reading. Not waivable by anyone in this chat: independent scene selection/replacement and human review (M56 rule recorded with Duy's decisions; spec "Do not trial A/B before C0 as budget-free selection"). So a qualified M56 catalog cannot be produced by Claude.
Online detection cost (T14 open risk): memoized world_vertices (key = repr of its 15 inputs; no -0.0/Fraction aliasing; test added) and the CompiledFixture digest (identity; deeply frozen by model._freeze). Diagnostic, whole maximal episode (1530 frames, fake backend): 116.4 s total online cost, worst tick 0.206 s (was about 300 s extrapolated), so it fits the 300 s outer deadline with room for native tick time. No kernel rewrite. Regression t14-streaming-memo-regression-20261021 running.
Native capability probe (charter native-probe-charter-20261021.md + addendum A1; own server CarlaUE4 port 2610, Town04, owned sync world; one pass per run, server stopped by the probe each time, CLEANED with no survivors each time). The probe exposed 4 REAL OwnedCarlaBackend defects, fixed with offline tests (test_runtime_owned 15 -> 20 passed):
 (1) apply() dropped manual_gear_shift/gear, so gear -1 never reached the server;
 (2) spawn() passed a batch INDEX as the sensor parent (SpawnActor expects an actor id). Now vehicles spawn first, then sensors attach to the real ids;
 (3) the clock: CARLA reports the fixed step as float32(0.05) = 0.05000000074505806, but the consumer requires dt == 1/20 exactly (reverse_intent REVERSE_CLOCK_INVALID etc.). Now exactly that float32 step gives dt 1/20 and simtime = origin + frames/20; any other step stays raw (fail safe); raw delta/elapsed kept;
 (4) sensors: the Python sensor object went out of scope, so CARLA dropped the stream (0 packets despite a real contact); and once kept alive, callbacks ran on CARLA's thread and collided with the exclusive RunStore writer lock. Now references are held, packets are queued, and they are drained on the runner thread at every tick and at stop.
 Also added: per-tick server control readback in raw frames.
Final probe run outputs/m56_native_probe_v1_20261021T133854Z: PROBE_COMPLETE.
 P1: 200 contiguous ticks, dt exactly 1/20.
 P2: A=147, B=148, collision sensor 149 attached to A; cleanup CLEANED, 0 survivors.
 P3: readback reverse True, gear -1, manual True for all ticks.
 P4: open_door/close_door OK; no door getter exists (no collider/clearance witness).
 P5: 10 collision packets, frame-correlated with owned ticks.
 P3b FEASIBILITY FINDING: at the frozen F03/F06 reverse throttle cap 0.25 the a2 reverses at only ~0.055 m/s steady (0.049/0.055/0.055 m/s at 2/5/10 s; 0.51 m in 10 s; never reaches the 0.5 m/s target; a 2 m reverse would take ~36 s), identical across runs. NOT tuned and the cap NOT changed (principle 6: no threshold change after seeing the number). It is a native blocker for F03/F06 reverse as frozen; it needs Duy, the only one who may invoke the objective-error exception.
Capability evidence only: not C0, not a fixture, not scene validation, not A/B; G3/G4/G5 NOT_GRANTED.

 (1) OwnedCarlaBackend could not drive a fixture at all (frames carried actors=()). Added bind(fixture, policy_for[, geometry_review]): fixture-bound ActorFrames (observed pose, velocity, bounding box; route station by the consumer's _metric_station; attributed command; server readback; stock plan queue), plan_refs, and stock intents through the reviewed create_stock_driver/apply_intent with a native _stock_factory (_NativeStock wraps the PINNED BasicAgent/LocalPlanner; the reviewed plan of {x,y,z} points becomes map waypoints with LANEFOLLOW).
 (2) No NPC priority witness: step_response requires npc_controls. The native witness is the stock LocalPlanner NPC's only observable priority input, the traffic light (RED_LIGHT if at a red light, else CLEAR), written per tick with frame-correlated refs; raw refs now carry (frame, time).
 (3) The runner never admitted the CAPTURE_START anchor the consumer replays with, so programs released at CAPTURE_START+delay never left their first phase. run_episode now anchors CAPTURE_START at the boundary (boundary frame_refs) and _commands keeps it out of the bounded reverse kernel, as the consumer does. Test test_runner_anchors_capture_start_like_the_consumer: RED (never released) -> GREEN.
 (4) float32 again: CARLA VehicleControl fields are float32. The stock validator rejected the agent's own clamp (steer -0.800000011920929 vs -0.8; also throttle/brake), so apply_intent now bounds against the float32 images (RED 3 failed -> GREEN 21). The consumer accepts a readback equal to the command or its float32 image only; the reverse throttle/brake caps compare against float32 images.
 (5) Automatic transmission: with manual_gear_shift False the server ignores the commanded gear and reads back the actual gear (HOLD gear 1 -> readback 0), so gear is not compared there; manual gear must match exactly.
 (6) The frame refs must hold exactly the evaluated observation {boot, world, frame, simtime, actors}; the adapter pointed them at raw server state (FRAME_OBSERVATION_CONTENT_MISMATCH). It now writes the observation record. In bound mode that record is the only per-tick record (no duplicate server-state file; ~13 ms per append_record).
 (7) W01 map identity: routes carry the OpenDRIVE sha256 but identity() reported the map NAME, so W01 always mismatched natively. identity() now reports the sha256 of the server's to_opendrive().
 Findings (not fixed in code): W01 requires the lower-case grants {'tick','observe'} while runtime admission uses upper-case op names (TICK, SPAWN...), so the engineering receipt grants both; an operator receipt must as well (vocabulary inconsistency to resolve in a future version). Spawn: CARLA refuses a spawn at rest height (collision); clearance 5-20 mm above rest spawns with settle peak 0.018-0.073 m/s (<= 0.1); >= 5 cm violates settle (measure m56_native_spawn_clearance_*). Real fixtures must take spawn z from a runtime rest-height query plus ~1 cm. The improvised +x route is straight for only ~90 m (cars hit the roadside at station ~90; route cut to 80 m).
 Expected review-gated reasons (by design: the runner never writes reviews): INITIALIZATION_INVALID ('binding': needs the INITIALIZATION review), W01_AUTHORITY_REVIEW_MISSING, W05_REVIEW_MISSING, GROUND/DELIVERY/CONTACT/MOTION proofs, METRIC_CONTEXT_MISSING.
 Open: wall time ~0.25 s per native tick (raw writes + online detection), so a 60 s episode overran the 300 s outer deadline (OUTER_TIMEOUT at 1204 frames). Measuring after the write reduction.
Engineering native A/B final run 2026-10-22 (outputs/m56_native_engineering_ab_20261022T070259Z, ENGINEERING-NOT-M56): full pipeline end to end; A ran its whole 60 s validation horizon within the 300 s outer deadline (bound mode writes only the observation + NPC witness: OUTER_TIMEOUT gone); TRIGGER detected online and authenticated by the consumer (A frame 130, B 1352); CONTROL_READBACK_MISMATCH, FRAME_OBSERVATION_CONTENT_MISMATCH, W01 identity mismatch and PHASE_EVENT_WITNESS_UNKNOWN all gone; 0 survivors. Remaining reasons: review-gated by design (INITIALIZATION, W01/W05 reviews, ground/motion/contact/delivery proofs, metric context) or scene semantics of the improvised scene (NPC ahead on the same road: no NPC_ENTRY/CLEAR/E_ENTRY/DESTINATION; B CONTINUOUS_YIELD_SPEED_FLOOR is the C yield guard). NATIVE FINDING FOR DUY (not changed, principle 6): NPC_UNPLANNED_REVERSE came from ONE station decrease of 0.82 mm (frame 149; physics jitter). The zero-tolerance monotonic-station rules (metrics ~l.430/~l.828) would flag almost any native run; a tolerance (e.g. the continuous-geometry uncertainty that other rules already subtract) would be a criterion change after seeing a number, so it is Duy's decision.
